Test evidence: `crates/xedit-session/tests/script_misc.rs` (6 tests, all green: the string-list surface including the `ValueFromIndex` write shape, `xejviScriptAdapter.pas`'s helpers and records with `GetRecordDefNames` over the loaded SSE definitions, the StrUtils/SysUtils/Math/Variants helpers, the full `TPerlRegEx` walk, the JSON surface with a save/load round trip, and the corpus's `JSON - Demo.pas`, which runs its first demo and stops at the JVCL `SysUtils` function `IntToStr` (step 8; the test then asserts that abort point, so it covers the demo's whole run once step 8 lands) -- the whole-log assertions of the kind step 10 will compare), plus 7 unit tests in the regex module; the four units' ledger marking and the whole workspace (`cargo test --workspace` green; `xedit script check` stays 150/150). One evaluator fix was needed for the surfaces: `assign_target`'s probe of an indexed target now uses upstream's fresh args object (Obj only, no index, `Indexed` off, `JvInterpreter.pas:6985`), so an `AddIGet`-only reader no longer answers the probe and the write lands in the `AddISet` entry with its index -- without it `sl.Values[name] := x` and `Obj.S['x'] := ...` mangled a character of the read string instead (the port's old behaviour wrote `gamma` -> `g3mma`). Owed/documented divergences: `SelectDirectory` raises the step-9 GUI-shim error headless; the JSON stream adapters, the time-zone bias of `UseUtcTime`, `D[]`'s parse and `jdtUtcDateTime` were closed by parity #30 (see the backlog results below); the `regex` crate had no lookaround/in-pattern backreferences/possessive quantifiers, `\d`/`\w`/`\s` were Unicode where PCRE was ASCII and `preNotBOL`/`preNotEOL`/`preNoAutoCapture` had no equivalent -- all closed by the parity backlog's #29 (`cargo xtask parity script --script "Regex probe" --phase run`, the `TPerlRegEx` probe of `crates/xtask/oracle/scripts/probes`, 222 lines equal on the release and the port over the empty master) (documented in the module), and offsets count characters where upstream counts UTF-8 bytes; `TwbVector`'s fields are doubles (the port has no `varSingle`); `CreateProcessWait` does not set the window's show state; `SetPrecisionMode`/`SetRoundMode` record without touching the FPU; `wbRemoveDuplicateStrings`' `TStringList.Find` quirk (a binary search whatever `Sorted` says) is reproduced. The two `TStringList` models the steps now carry -- step 5's `xedit_script::strings::ScriptStringList` (the `StringList` trait the step-5/6 host functions fill through `Value::string_list_mut`) and step 7's `script_strings::StringListObject` (the script-visible `TStringList.Create` object with the DelimitedText/ValueFromIndex/set-operation surface) -- are bridged: `StringListObject` stores its lines as bytes and implements that trait, so `GetMasters`-style fills write the same list the script reads (the tests of both steps cover it). Owed to step 8 (the JVCL `Classes` unit, which owns `TStringList`): the two object types become one, and the members this slice still lacks (`AddObject`/`InsertObject` as script entries, `CommaText`, `Names`/`Values`/`Objects`, `LoadFromStream`, ...) come with it; a list object created by the resource adapter's `TwbFastStringList.Create` (step 5's own model) does not answer this surface's methods until then. Left for step 9: `xejviScriptAdapterMiscVcl.pas` untouched (all 103 entries, including the `AddClass` surface and the three `AddHandler`s); its map entry still describes that split, and `SelectDirectory`'s error is the seam the form shim replaces. No parity number is claimed here: the oracle comparison is step 10's (the corpus `JSON - Demo.pas` run is asserted against the *expected* log, not against a recorded oracle log).
8. **Core JVCL units** (Sonnet 5.5, after 4): the 313 `AddFunction` of the 14 registered JVCL units, on the Delphi RTL semantics `xedit-core::delphi` already carries where they overlap (numbers, text): `System`, `SysUtils`, `Math`, `Classes`, `Dialogs`, `Windows`, `Graphics`, `Controls`, `Buttons`, `StdCtrls`, `ComCtrls`, `ExtCtrls`, `Forms`, `Menus`.
   - *Notes (branch p6-step8, 2026-10-10).* **Scope refinement.** The step ports the non-VCL RTL backbone of the 15 units `xejviScriptAdapter.pas` registers at `:356-371`: `JvInterpreter_System.pas`, `JvInterpreter_SysUtils.pas`, `JvInterpreter_Math.pas`, `JvInterpreter_Classes.pas` and `JvInterpreterFm.pas`'s own four registrations. The ten VCL-backed units (`_Dialogs`, `_Windows`, `_Graphics`, `_Controls`, `_Buttons`, `_StdCtrls`, `_ComCtrls`, `_ExtCtrls`, `_Forms`, `_Menus`) stay step 9's, because their headless behaviour needs the form shim; where a unit of this step would delegate into the VCL, it routes through a small `Vcl` seam trait (`script_rtl::Vcl`), which step 9 implements. Headless the seam's `NoVcl` default answers `<function>: forms are not available in this build`, and `JvInterpreterFm.pas`'s four registrations (`JvInterpreterRunFormModal`, `JvInterpreterRunForm`, `JvInterpreterMakeForm`, `JvInterpreterRunUnit`) go through it. **Sync scope.** `cargo xtask sync` scanned only `xEdit/JvI/`, so the JVCL units were not in the ledger at all; `crates/xtask/src/main.rs` now reads the registered JVCL files by name (`JVCL_UNITS`, `External/jvcl/jvcl/run/`, plus the two interpreter core files the map already tracked) into the same registration scan. The ledger grew from 986 to 3659 script entries (the 15 JVCL units contribute 3222, of which this step's five are 573), and the map gained the 15 JVCL unit entries (the five of this step `ported`, the ten VCL ones `pending` for step 9). **What landed.** `crates/xedit-session/src/script_rtl/` (mod, `system.rs`, `sysutils.rs`, `math.rs`, `classes.rs`, `fm.rs`, plus the new `format.rs` -- the Delphi `Format` family's `%`-conversion language, which no earlier phase needed -- and `datetime.rs` -- the TDateTime codec and `DateTimeToString` specifiers): 76 live registrations of `System` (the `TObject` methods, the `Var*` family with the Delphi variant type codes, `Length`/`Copy`/`Pos`/`Delete`/`Insert`/`SetLength`/`High`/`Low`/the array element helpers, the math functions through `nif_math`, `Randomize`/`Random` as the RTL's LCG, the 24 constants), 210 of `SysUtils` (the `Exception` family with the Delphi hierarchy, the string/file/path/`Str*` families, the `Format`/`FloatToStr*`/`StrToFloat*` family, the date family, `TSearchRec` with `FindFirst`/`FindNext`/`FindClose`, the locale helpers, the `fm*`/`fa*` constants), 67 of `Math` (the `Math.pas` functions with Delphi's formula semantics -- `Hypot`'s scaling, `Power` through `IntPower`, `Ln`-quotient logs -- and the eleven constants incl. `MinExtended` = `MinDouble` on the 64-bit build; the upstream `ArcCotH`-binds-`ArcCot` quirk kept), 191 of `Classes` and the 4 of `JvInterpreterFm`. **Reuse.** `xedit-core::delphi` for the number/text semantics, `nif_math` for the FPU transcendentals, `xedit_io::encoding::ansi_compare_text` for `AnsiCompareText`, the phase 5 Sniff `processor` helpers for `TStrings.CommaText`, and -- the integration point that mattered -- the `TList`/`TStrings` objects phase 5 introduced for the adapter signatures (`xedit_script::lists::ScriptList`, `xedit_script::strings::ScriptStringList`), whose traits this step extends with the member set the `Classes` unit needs (`set`/`delete_at`/`insert`/`exchange`/`move_item`/`remove`/`pack`/`resize`/`capacity`/`reserve`, `set_object`/`exchange`/`move_line`/`set_sorted`/`set_duplicates`/`sort`/`compare_strings`/`capacity`/`reserve`), so a `TStringList` a host function fills (`GetMasters`, `wbStringListInString`, `GetAssetsList`) is the same value the script reads, `TwbFastStringList` is its subclass, and the `ScriptLists` seam of the run state now appends through the same `Add`. `IntToStr`/`BoolToStr`-era expectations of step 5's tests were updated (there is no `BoolToStr` in the oracle at all; the boolean concatenation reads `True`/`False`), and `sl.Strings[0]` works, so the step-5 test that used the call form reads the indexed property. **Ledger.** Every live registration of the five units is bound and marked `covered`/`script.run` (548 entries: 76 + 210 + 67 + 191 + 4); 24 commented-out upstream registrations and `faVolumeID` (behind `{$IFNDEF COMPILER8_UP}`) are `excluded` with their lines. A unit test (`script_rtl::tests::every_ledger_entry_of_the_jvcl_units_is_registered`) rebuilds the ledger keys from the adapter's registration list and asserts set equality with the ledger's live entries of the five units, so a registration cannot go missing or be invented silently. `cargo xtask check` at the tip after the rebases onto steps 5 and 7: script 3659 entries, 2233 pending; units 172 tracked, 81 pending. **Tests.** 10 unit tests in the step's modules (the `Format` conversions and pictures, `IntToHex`/`StrToInt`/`StrToFloat` texts, the TDateTime codec, the `DateTimeToString` specifiers, `StrToDate*` with the format settings, DOS file dates, the registration counts and the ledger equality) plus `the_jvcl_rtl_units_run_a_script` in `crates/xedit-session/tests/script.rs`, one script whose ~47 `AddMessage` lines each pin a different RTL function (System string/array/number, SysUtils text/format/date/path/exception, Math, `TStringList` incl. `Values`/`Names`/`CommaText`/`Text`/`Sorted`/`Duplicates`/`SaveToFile`/`LoadFromStream`, `TList`, `TFileStream`, `FormatFloat`), and `the_corpus_scripts_that_need_the_jvcl_units_run`, five 4.1.5q corpus scripts (`List perk entry points usage.pas`, `List LOD objects and meshes.pas`, `Check for invalid alternate textures.pas`, `Worldspace scale refs position.pas`, `Skyrim - List used scripts.pas`) that now run headless to their `Done:` line. `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` and `cargo xtask check` green at the tip. **Owed / notes.** (1) The six custom variant type codes (`varObject`, `varRecord`, `varClass`, `varPointer`, `varSet`, `varArray`) are runtime-allocated `TCustomVariantType` codes upstream; the port assigns fixed ones (`0x0103`-`0x0108`) and no constant is registered for them, so only `VarType()` of such a value could observe the difference; `VarIsArray` answers true for a native variant array only (the `and varArray` bit test, which the `Length`/`High`/`Low`/`SetLength` handlers complement with the custom-type test). (2) `SetLength` of an interpreter-array variable raises `Array to array assignment` where upstream's `var` write-back does the same (`JvInterpreterVarAssignment`); a fixed-size array in a `var` declaration is the shape the dialect supports -- no corpus script calls `SetLength` at all. (3) `TStream.Read`/`FileRead`/`FileWrite` pass their buffer as a `var` string (upstream reads/writes the variant's raw payload memory, garbage by construction); `TList.List` is the nil pointer, `TStrings.StringsAdapter` read is `Null`. (4) `Beep` is silent, `ShowException` writes nothing, `DiskFree`/`DiskSize` answer -1 (no volume API), `GetLocaleStr` answers the en-US values, `ExceptObject`/`ExceptAddr` are nil outside a handler (the `except on E` binding is a script variable), `WithEvents`-style RTTI is out of scope. (5) `TObject.ClassInfo` is `nil` and `InstanceSize` 0 (no RTTI in the value model). (6) No parity number is claimed: the oracle comparison of these units is step 10's, and the corpus runs above are the port's own green runs, not an oracle diff. (7) Left for step 9: the ten VCL units, the form shim behind the `Vcl` seam, `TMemIniFile` (registered by `xejviScriptAdapterMiscVcl.pas`), and the `Classes` RTTI members the form units need.
9. **VCL form shim** (Sonnet 5.5, after 4): the `AddClass` surface (59 in the adapters) and the form units: headless, a script that constructs or shows a form fails with a clear error naming the class and the script; the shim is the seam the GUI phase fills (filled by issue #26: `script_vcl::interactive`'s headless object model and the recorded-answer bridge). `ScriptCanProcessElement` and the selection hooks behave as upstream.

`TGroupBox`/`TPage`/`TNotebook`/`THeader` are `TCustomControl`s, `TStatusBar` a `TWinControl` with its own `Canvas`, `TScrollBar` a `TWinControl`), the 782 `AddConst` rows with the *captured* oracle values (the `cl*` colours incl. the system colours' `$FF000000`-form codes, the Windows `MB_*`/`VK_*`, the `Ord(...)` enums, the `se*`/`so*` of the misc unit), the 1,127 member rows (820 `Get`, 288 `Set`, 11 `IGet`, 8 `ISet`), the 44 unit functions, the 41 `AddHandler` rows and the `Menus` key-name tables (`ShortCutToText` names 127 codes, 108 distinct names parse back, both captured); `windows.rs` is `JvInterpreter_Windows.pas`'s value half -- the `TPoint`/`TRect` records with `Point`/`Rect`/`Bounds` and the `TopLeft`/`BottomRight` accessors, `AddExtUnit('Windows')` and `MessageBox` as the unit's one `AddExtFun` (`user32`, through the adapter's `external_call` seam); `helpers.rs` the 13 unit functions that answer headless (`Controls`' `GetShortHint`/`GetLongHint`/`CursorToString`/`StringToCursor`/`CursorToIdent`/`IdentToCursor` with the cursor table, `Forms`' `IsAccel`/`KeysToShiftState`/`KeyDataToShiftState`, `Menus`' `ShortCut`/`ShortCutToKey`/`ShortCutToText`/`TextToShortCut`); `misc_vcl.rs` is the whole `xejviScriptAdapterMiscVcl.pas` (its 12 classes, 5 constants, the `IOUtils` `TDirectory`/`TFile` records with `GetDirectories`/`GetFiles` -- full paths, the `System.Masks` wildcards incl. `[...]` -- `ReadAllText`/`WriteAllText`, `THashedStringList.Create` and the `IniFiles` classes over `IniFileState`/`IniFileRef`: the `System.IniFiles` parse rules, `Read*`/`Write*`/`SectionExists`/`ReadSections` with the `Strings.Clear` the RTL does, `WriteBool` as `1`/`0`, `WriteFloat` through `FloatToStr`, `TMemIniFile.UpdateFile`'s section-per-block CRLF text, a `TIniFile` writing through on every change). *The seam.* Step 8's `script_rtl::Vcl` was extended in place with `member`/`function` and the `MemberKind` enum, and its `NoVcl` carries the script name and refuses every call with `[EVclUnavailable] <class>.<member> is not available without a GUI (script "<name>")` (`VCL_ERROR_CLASS`); `script.rs` builds `NoVcl::new(<script name>)` per run, and the GUI phase implements the trait -- the objects of a `TForm.Create` member call, the dialog functions, the four `JvInterpreterFm` form functions -- without touching the registrations. The surface (class name plus member) is what step 10's classification greps for, so a form script's abort names `TForm`, an interactive one's the dialog. *Ledger.* All 2,206 entries of the eleven units are marked: 996 `covered` / `command = "script.run"` (the classes, the 782 constants, the records and accessors, the 41 handlers, the 16 headless unit functions of the ten units and the 21 `IniFiles`/`IOUtils` gets of the misc unit) and 1,210 `excluded` with the shim note (the 1,125 members, the 28 unit functions that are dialogs, `MessageBox`, the three `TBinary*`/`TBytesStream` constructors, `TRegistryIniFile.Create`, `TBitmap.SetSize`); `cargo xtask sync`'s registration scanner gained the `AddRecSet`/`AddExtFun` kinds and the `recget`-shaped key of `AddRecSet`, and the eleven map entries are `ported`. *Evidence.* Two check scripts run side by side on the oracle (hidden GUI, `cargo xtask parity script --phase parse`) and on the port with **identical logs**: `vcl_helpers.pas` (50 `OK` lines -- the shortcut codec incl. the `ShortCut(300, 0)` = 0 quirk and the `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+A` word order, the hint pair, `IsAccel`, the shift-state sets probed through `ShortCut`, the cursor table incl. `crNone` not being an ident, and every `TPoint`/`TRect` operation) and `vcl_ini.pas` (24 `OK` lines -- the ini reads, the section fillers, `TMemIniFile.UpdateFile`'s bytes, the `TDirectory`/`TFile` helpers); `crates/xedit-session/tests/script_vcl.rs` (6 tests) runs both scripts and two corpus scripts -- `Find records.pas` aborts at `TForm.Create` (the corpus classes it `form`) and `Add prefix or suffix to Editor ID.pas` at `MessageDlg` (it classes it `interactive`) -- plus `TComboBox.Create` and `StringToCursor('crNone')`'s `EConvertError`; 7 module tests cover the `NoVcl` texts, the census counts, the class parents, the ledger agreement (every `covered` entry is registered, nothing registered is unlisted) and the `IniFileState`/*Run-phase parity.* With the step in, `cargo xtask parity script --phase run --script Bookmark1.pas` and `--script Bookmark2.pas` are **`equal`** (1 file, 1 log line each: the `Bookmark.ini` the scripts write is byte-identical between the oracle and the port, and a `Mode: Silent` script's whole log is the closing line -- 2 of 2 cases), the first `equal` run cases of the phase. Getting there took one step-4 fix in `script.rs`: a `Mode: Silent` script's `wbProgressLock` is now on *before* the `Start:` line (the oracle's log holds neither `Start:` nor `Done:` for one, which the earlier ordering printed). `--script "DDS - Find"` compares 5 of 5 log lines and its files byte-equal but the script's path text differs: the oracle scans its private data folder, the port the real game `Data` (the parity recipe hands the port no private data folder for a case without `save`) -- step 10's open item, not a script behaviour difference. *Findings.* `TDirectory` models. *Findings.* (1) The oracle answers `VarType('x')` = `$102` (`varUString`); the port's `_System` `VarType` returned `$100`, and the duplicate `Variants`/`System` registrations left the winner to the sorted list's binary search, which any new registration could flip (this step's 44 functions did) -- fixed in `script_rtl/system.rs` with a `DELPHI_VAR_USTRING` constant and the oracle probe recorded in the comment, which also pins the answer for step 10. (2) The oracle raises `Type mismatch` for `'x' + 5` and `'x' + True`; the port's `+` converts instead (`crates/xedit-script/src/values.rs` asserts `a1`/`xTrue`) -- a step-3 divergence step 10 should rule on (no corpus script does it; one that did aborts on the oracle). (3) The port resolves a record method through the type name (`TFile.ReadAllText(...)`); the oracle needs a variable of the record type -- an over-acceptance in step 3/5's dispatch. (4) The upstream `SetExpression1` quirk is kept: an integer element of a set literal contributes its *value* (`[0, 3]` is 3, not 9), so the shift-state checks go through `ShortCut`. *Owed.* Filled by issue #26 (`parity-26`): the seam's headless implementation is `script_vcl::interactive` (the object model with properties, events and synthetic handles; the dialogs answered from the recorded inputs; `script.run`'s `answers`/`pending_interaction` and `script.resume`), the dialogs and `MessageBox` are answered there, and the eleven units' 1,210 seam-refused entries are reclassified to `covered`/`presentation`; still owed to phase 7: rendering the 54 `presentation` members, firing assigned event handlers from a real message loop, the `TBinary*` stream objects' construction (their members are the `Classes` unit's), and the four `JvInterpreterFm` form-runner functions; the abort *line* still carries the port's `External error in unit 'X' on line N :` wrapper where the oracle prints the bare message (the step-4/step-10 owed item, unchanged here). Gates at the tip: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (all green) and `cargo xtask check` (script 3,663 entries, 31 pending; units 172 tracked, 70 pending).
10. **Parity: `cargo xtask parity script`** (Opus 5.5): drives the oracle GUI hidden over the corpus: parsing (the oracle compiles, the port checks) and the headless corpus's runs, comparing the files written, the message log lines, the exit status, and the plugin bytes of the scripts that save. The parity batch #28 added a probe phase (`--phase probe`): 880 one-behaviour scripts of `crates/xtask/oracle/scripts/probes/` run on both sides over a two-record probe master and are compared as a run; 880 of 880 equal (841 `equal`, 32 `known-difference` accepted by `probes.json`, 7 `equal-error`; on the tree rebased onto #22, #23, #30, #32 and #40). Results and the case list in the Status.
    *Notes (branch p6-step10, 2026-10-10)* — `cargo xtask parity script` landed in `crates/xtask/src/parity/script.rs` (dispatched from `parity.rs`, which also names it in the USAGE); `gui.rs` grew `Until::ScriptEnd` and `GuiRun::run_script` (a corpus run ends at `You can close this application now.`, at `Aborted: Applying script`, or at a dialog — collected as a class, not a failure — and closes the main form for subsequent saves) and `GuiRun::extra_args`. Cases: `crates/xtask/oracle/scripts/parse.json` (every `.pas` of `XEDIT_SCRIPTS`, one GUI run each, over an empty synthetic game master the harness writes, so parsing needs no game data) and `crates/xtask/oracle/scripts/runs.json` (117 form-free scripts: 109 headless + 8 interactive listed but deferred; per case a game, plugins, `build_refs`, `close_after` and the `save` plugins). Oracle classification of a compile: `done` / `aborted` (the script ran and raised) / `interactive` (a dialog; a window class of the script's own proves the compile) / `compile-error` (a task dialog without an `Applying script` line, which `ApplyScript` posts after the compile); runs compare the files the script wrote (`out/` = `wbOutputPath`, `script/` = the run folder = `ScriptsPath()`, `program/`, `data/`), the message-log lines of the script section (`Start: Applying script`..the closing line, no times; for a `Mode: Silent` script the lines without a time prefix), and the bytes of the `save` plugins (captured from the GUI's `<name>.save.<time>`, whose rename is queued for a shutdown the harness never reaches); cached per case under `<MODE>-oracle-script/<stem>.<key>/` keyed by recipe + script + used units + plugin hashes + exe name. Selection semantics recorded from `DoRunScript`/`ApplyScript`: headless the selection is every root node of the nav tree, each subtree walked from its last descendant to the node itself. Port side wired (`xedit script check <file>` per parse case; `xedit script run <scripts>\<name> --scripts <dir> --output <dir> [--build-refs] [--save <plugin>] --allow-edit-game-master`, probed once) and reported `port-pending` until steps 2 and 4 land: no faked results, no port code in this step. Owed to step 2: `script check` must resolve identifiers against the host's registered units and classes (see the refusals below). Owed to step 4: the three switches the oracle side stands in for (`--build-refs`, `--save`, `--allow-edit-game-master`, the last of which mirrors the GUI's `-IKnowIllBreakMyGameWithThis` that lets a script edit the game master of the private copy — without it every mutating case aborts on its first edit), `result.messages` as the message log, and `--output` for `wbOutputPath`. Oracle results: **parse**, all 150 `.pas` in 4m50s at `--jobs 4`, with step 2's `script check` in place — **150 of 150 equal** (149 `equal` + 1 `known-difference`, exit 0): the oracle compiled 148 (87 `done`, 51 `interactive`, 10 `aborted`) and refused two, of which `xEditAPI.pas` is the one `parse.json` accepts with its reason (JvInterpreter 4.1.5q faults compiling the declaration-only API file: an access violation in `InterpretConst`/`Expression`, `JvInterpreter.pas:7426`); the second refusal was a false read the classification now avoids — `Bookmark.pas` compiled and showed its bookmark list from its main block, which `Compile` runs before `ApplyScript` posts `Applying script` (a script's own `MessageDlg`, caption `Information`, differs from the application's exception dialog, whose caption carries the GUI's `4.1.5q`; three scripts' Windows `Open`/`Save As` dialogs are also `#32770` and their paths once carried that version inside the dialog text). Probes recorded: a syntax error inside a routine body does not fail the compile at all (the compile skips bodies; it raises at run time), an error outside one does (`Error in unit 'X' on line N : ...`), an undeclared type in a `var` section is accepted. **run**, with step 4's `script run` in place: 107 of the 117 cases in 12m03s at `--jobs 3` (the seven slowest skipped with `--skip`, two New Vegas cases skipped for an emptied data folder) — **0 equal, 78 `different`, 29 `oracle-interactive`**: the port stops on a name its host has not registered yet in almost every script (`Signature` 19, `TStringList` 18, `IsMaster` 4, `TList` 3, `TwbFastStringList`, `RecordByFormID`, `GetIsEditable`, `SetElementEditValues`, `Pred`, `SetJDOLineBreak`), `SetESL.pas` fails the port's body parse where the oracle compiles and runs it, the filter scripts log `Start: Applying Filter` against the port's `Script terminated itself, Result=1`, a `Mode:` script's lines are suppressed by the oracle and printed by the port, and the abort text differs in shape; `_newscript_.pas` is the live evidence of step 5's `FullPath` finding (the oracle logs `Processing:  \ [00] Skyrim.esm \ [111] GRUP Top "REVB" \ [9] IntRoomWoodMediumReverb [REVB:000C5B6A]`), no case reaches the single-precision print yet, and step 4's owed abort/`Processed Records` comparisons are exercised (869,712 records for Skyrim LE's master with `TESV.exe`, 962,397 for Skyrim SE's with the hardcoded file, 1,549,451 for `Fallout4.esm` with its own); the earlier full pass of all 117 (94m41s) and its fixups (per-game load, slow-case timeouts) are in the commits — four report scripts, `Bookmark1` (`Mode: Silent`, `Bookmark.ini` captured as `script/Bookmark.ini`) and two Skyrim LE save cases (`Skyrim - Add shadow bias to lights` done, 869,717 records, `saved/Skyrim.esm` 247,847,404 bytes; `Skyrim - Remove frequence variance from sound descriptors` aborted after ten seconds with its partial edits saved, 247,803,004 bytes); the close writes its save as `<name>.save.<time>` and queues the rename for a shutdown the harness never reaches, which the capture handles.
    *Notes (branch p6-rp-fix, 2026-10-10)* -- the run-parity fixups, each verified against upstream at the pin and re-checked with real runs. **(1) The walk** (`crates/xedit-session/src/script.rs`'s `flatten`): a main record's children are its adjacent child group and a parented group whose label is the preceding record's FormID is that record's hidden sibling node (`vstNavInitNode`, `xeMainForm.pas:20228-20347`), so every record is reached exactly once; the old walk descended child groups through both paths and over-delivered `Process` calls 2.2-2.7x (the 956b151 re-run: `_newscript_.pas` over Skyrim.esm 2,312,470 against the oracle's 869,712; `Undelete and Disable References.pas` 2,312,467 vs 869,712; `Update records form version.pas` 298,926 vs 137,343). Port-side single-case runs at the fixup tip (`M:\projects\xedit-parity-scratch-p6-rp-fix\p6rp-*.json`): `Undelete` `Processed Records: 869712` in the oracle's four-line log shape; `_newscript_` 869712 processed with 869,715 full message lines, line 1 `Processing:  \ [00] Skyrim.esm \ [111] GRUP Top "REVB" \ [9] IntRoomWoodMediumReverb [REVB:000C5B6A]`; `Update records form version` 869712 processed. **(2) Script-visible names**: `wbDisplayShorterNames` is on for a run (`xeMainForm.pas:5152`) -- `Name`/`FullPath`/messages give `EditorID "Full Name" [SIG:FormID]`; The threshold is synthetic and sized against the test harness's 2 MiB debug threads (measured: the runaway probe overflows between 72 and 80 frames, ≈26 KB of native stack per interpreted call; the CLI debug binary's PE header reserves 16 MiB). The review's `Rec.pas`, run through the port CLI on 2026-10-10, aborts with `Exception in unit Rec line 1: [EStackOverflow] Stack overflow` + `Aborted: Applying script "Rec"` instead of `thread 'main' has overflowed its stack`. The parser got the same guard (`interpreter::MAX_NESTING_DEPTH` 128, error code 52 `Stack overflow`; measured: 128 nesting levels pass in the harness, 188 overflow; upstream's compile overflows outside `ApplyScript`'s catch, so it has no catchable shape): both caps are the one recorded divergence of this landing. **(2) The `etSubRecord` walk.** `flatten` no longer descends a main record's element container -- a record's node children are its adjacent parented child group alone (`xeMainForm.pas:20285-20288`) -- and the review's probes re-run through the CLI against SSE `Update.esm` on 2026-10-10: `ScriptProcessElements := [etSubRecord]` processes **0** (`subrecords=0`; the pre-fix walk delivered 4,373,854) and `[etMainRecord]` stays **886,200** (`records=886200`), the review's oracle figure.     *Notes (branch parity-23, 2026-10-15, issue #23)* — the edit-index, reference-update and EDID-script batch, each cause read at the pin and witnessed against the oracle. **(1) The EDID script's bytes.** The oracle's save of `Add Prefix Plus Name To EDID.pas` (271,172,991 bytes) was diffed record by record against the port's (270,481,660): 704,214 records differed in the EDID text (the port's `Copy(s, 0, n)` dropped a character: the RTL clamps the index to 1 and keeps the count, `script_rtl/system.rs`), 697 `KYWD`, 2 `LCRT`, 69 `AACT` and 82 `ACTI` had only their `EDID` in the oracle's save where the port kept (and completed) their subrecords, and 90 `AVIF` had `CNAM` 0 in the oracle's. The record loss is upstream's quick init: `GetEditorID` runs `Init` under `mrsQuickInit`, and a record whose subrecords all lie before `QuickInitLimit` never reaches the early exit, so the tail of `Init` runs — `AfterLoad`, the required members as an internal edit (which invalidates the storage) — and `DoReset(True)` then releases the elements; the next init's `GetDataBasePtr` collapses the invalidated storage from the elements it has (the unsaved record header alone), so the record is empty and `if not Assigned(cntElements) then Exit` skips its tail. The port's quick init now creates no header or contained-in element, runs the tail when the limit was not passed (`init_main_record` reports it), and `get_editor_id`/`get_full_name` answer `<EditorID not yet available: init still running>` inside the quick init as upstream. *Superseded 2026-10-16 (branch `fix-sse-filters`, below):* the record loss is an upstream bug that loses data and is no longer reproduced; the quick init collapses the invalidated storage from its elements before it releases them, `collapse_storage_in_init` (the collapse from nothing) is left for a storage some other release left invalid, the fixture case reads every record through `ElementByName` first, and `Add Prefix Plus Name To EDID.pas` is a `known-difference` with the reason in `runs.json`. The AVIF `CNAM` is its `wbAVIFSkillAfterLoad`, which upstream runs lazily at the save: `TwbDataContainer.WriteToStreamInternal` reads `GetDataSize` (an init) before it decides what to write, so `data_container_write_to_stream` now reads the data after that init. The hardcoded file's player reference carries the required `PLYR` member (7) and sits among the groups by the group order (`TwbFile.Add` sorts them), which the walk order of the last records showed. Fixture: `tests/fixtures/parity/edid-quick-init.esm` with `crates/xtask/oracle/scripts/probes/edid-quick-init.pas` (a `master` case of `runs.json`: the element lists before and after the edit, the AVIF category, `Copy`, `StrictDelimiter`), `equal` (59 log lines, the saved bytes), and the unit test `the_edid_quick_init_probe_gives_the_oracle_lines` pins the oracle's lines. **(2) The EditorID index.** `UpdateKeys` is ported (`mr_index_keys` with `mrsIndexKeysActive`, `IndexKeys::changed_keys`, `FileImpl::update_index_keys` with `flUpdateChangedKeysInIndices`'s `Duplicate Key in Index "..." Existing Record: ... New Record: ...` line, `DeactivateIndexKeys` on removal), called from `ElementChanged` and `SetParentModified`; `Replace text in EDID and FULL.pas` is `equal` (675 lines). Upstream's quirks hold: a record renamed onto a taken key is reported and not indexed, and the key it leaves is not re-taken by the other record. **(3) UpdateRefs.** The `is_running_here` guard is gone: `UpdateRefs` builds the references while the record's own init changes it, walking the elements built so far as `TwbContainer.BuildRef` does. **(4) References from the cache.** `runs.json` cases take `refcache`: both sides first load with a no-op script and the reference build (the oracle writes its `-C:` files, the port its `--cache-path`), then run the case over those files; `probes/refs-edit.pas` over `tests/fixtures/parity/refs-edit.esm` (600 keywords, above `wbCacheRecordsThreshold`) reads the referenced-by lists before and after a replaced, an added and a removed keyword and the index lookups through the group and the file around renames, a removed EDID and an added and removed record: `equal` with and without the cache (82 lines, the saved bytes). **(5) Refused edits.** The script bindings `Add`, `AddElement`, `SetToDefault`, the master routines, the module flags, `SetLoadOrderFormID` and `SetIsDeleted` propagate a refusal as upstream's bare `Exception` instead of swallowing it, so `Replace model file name.pas` aborts with the oracle's `DefaultImpactDataSet [IPDS:00000276] can not be edited` (its `Male World Model` entry reaches `TwbMainRecord.Add` of the hardcoded record); the port's earlier `Model can not be edited.` came from `TStrings.DelimitedText` ignoring `StrictDelimiter` (23 fields for 11), fixed with the parser of `SetDelimitedText`. `ElementAssign(..., nil, ...)` took `nil` (`Value::Pointer(0)`) for a missing source and did nothing. An `on E: Exception` handler now sees a host exception with its class (`Exception` descends from the registered `TObject`, so `E.ClassName` resolves, and `E is <class>` tests the class the host named) and its message (`E.Message` read the raised object's field alone, so a refused edit reached the handler with an empty message). **(6) The double init** of a definition-made subrecord is upstream's: `TwbSubRecord.Create` inits over nil data and `SetToDefaultInternal` resets and inits again (`DoReset(True)` then `DoInit(True)`), which `create_new` mirrors; the master-update path keeps its `CollapseStorage` approximation (the collapsed bytes without a rebuild of the elements), which `oracle-edit` confirms. Gates and parity at the tip (2026-10-15, commit `4085008`): `cargo fmt --all --check` exit 0; `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` exit 0; `cargo test --workspace` exit 0 (54 test binaries; the one `the_perl_regex_surface_works` elapsed-time flake seen under load at 16:14 did not recur); `cargo xtask check` exit 0; the parity counts are in the Status paragraph.

    *Notes (branch parity-24, 2026-10-16, issue #24)* — the worldspace-move, changing-tree-walk and topic-sorting batch, each port read at the pin and witnessed against the oracle. **(1) The contained-in relocation.** `TwbContainedInElement.DoAfterSet` in full (`implementation/undelete.rs`): the value of the record's contained-in element (its `Worldspace`, `Cell`, `Topic` or `Quest`) is the owner, resolved as a load order FormID to the record of the file and deep-copied into the file from another file; the record moves to the children group of the owner — the cell children case (8/9/10 by the persistent, visible-when-distant and `wbVWDInTemporary` flags), the topic and quest children cases, and the worldspace case that re-creates the 4- and 5-group chain of the new worldspace under the old block and sub-block labels, removes the emptied old block and world groups and adds the record to the new sub-block group; a missing owner restores the old label in the element data and `cieLockCount` keeps `ContainerChanged` off while the relocation runs; the element's `DoAfterSet` hook runs it from the value setter and `assign_contained_in` shares the same function, with `TwbMainRecord.EnsureChildGroup` ported along. `SetElementEditValues(e, 'Cell', Name(cell))` — the right-cells script's call shape — resolves because `Name()` carries the bracketed FormID upstream's `FromEditValue` parses. `TwbMainRecord.DoAfterSet`'s tail is ported too: `eGeneration` on `ElementBase`, bumped by `element_set_modified` as `TwbElement.SetModified` does, `mrPositionGeneration` on the record and the `UpdateCellChildGroup` call a placed record whose `DATA\Position` changed needs, so the position-shift scripts move their references between the cells. `TwbMainRecord.Delete` is ported (contents and references dropped, the deleted flag set with partial-form and compressed cleared, the contained-in element and a fresh record header again, the FO4 base record link, `CollapseStorage`; no port front end calls it yet, upstream's only caller is `DeleteInto`, and it carries an `allow(dead_code)` note). **(2) The live-tree walk.** `script.run` steps over the live tree as `ApplyScriptToSelection` steps over `vstNav` with `GetLast`/`GetPrevious`: `crates/xedit-session/src/script.rs`'s selection hands the run loop its roots and `NavigationSelection::live_walk` descends `last_node` and steps `previous_node` (the last node of the previous sibling's subtree, or the parent) over `node_children` — a main record's children are its child group's elements alone and a parented group whose label is the FormID of the record before it is hidden, as `vstNavInitNode` builds them — computed before each `Process` call; a node a `Process` removed processes nothing (upstream's `NodeData.Element := nil`) and the walk continues from the predecessor captured before the call (upstream's freed node). The oracle's re-visits come from the same live tree with the GUI's `NavUpdate` running between `Process` calls (`wbTick` pumps the GUI every 500 ms; `NavCleanUpCollapsedNodeChildren` deletes the children of every initialized, not-expanded node and `NavCheckForChanges` re-inits changed nodes outside the game master and the hardcoded file, and `vstNavInitChildren` re-appends changed or missing elements at the end) — recorded, not reproduced, because the fixture probes show the effects are pump-timing-bound. **(3) The official-file name caches.** `mrName` (read and, for the records of official files, set by `GetName`) and `mrShortName` (`GetShortNameInternal`, when `wbNoFullInShortName` allows it) are ported with upstream's clears (relevant subrecord changes in `ElementChanged` and `RemoveElement`, the localization invalidation) and deliberately NOT cleared by a FormID change, so a renamed record keeps printing its stale `[SIG:FormID]` as the oracle does. **(4) The postponed topic sort.** `esEndingUpdate` around `UpdateEnded`, `gsSortPostponed`/`gsSortForcedPostponed` with `sort_with_force`, the group `NotifyChangedInternal` (the `gsSorting` guard and the `gsSorted` clear of a `wbSortINFO` topic group) and the sort of a topic group at the end of `TwbGroupRecord.MastersUpdated` — the PNAM ordering after a master or FormID change. **(5) Witnesses.** `tests/fixtures/parity/walk-churn.esm` (1,472 bytes; two worldspaces with the exterior blocks nested in the world children group as the real files nest them, a persistent and a temporary exterior cell with children and references, an interior cell, a `STAT` and a `DIAL` with a three-response PNAM chain) and the five run cases over it — `probes/walk-remove.pas` (44 lines), `walk-move.pas` (45: the moved `REFR 0000080A` is re-visited where the oracle re-visits it), `walk-cell-move.pas` (44: the persistent cell moves between the worldspaces and its children group keeps its references, the reload read back), `walk-shift.pas` (44: the position-shift shape of `Worldspace displacement`, cells and references shifting with one `RemoveNode`), `topic-renumber.pas` (52: the renumbered chain with the stale name) — all `equal`, saved bytes included, plus the new `oracle-edit` sequence `sse-update-topic.json` (Update.esm renumbered from `000A00` with `build_refs`) `equal` (input 19,121,864 bytes, saved 18,466,424 on both sides). The three named scripts at the tip (`cargo xtask parity script --phase run`, cached oracle): `Worldspace scale refs position` `equal` (the abort at its line 50 and the saved bytes compare), `Put worldspace references in the right cells` `equal` (764,713 lines and the saved bytes, its walk count 869,713 reproduced), `Worldspace displacement` `different`: the oracle's `Processed Records` 962,397 against the port's 1,329,579 with the saved bytes at 193,949,519 and 193,801,757 — the port over-visits where the shifts, the re-homes and the re-visits feed each other in the real file and the smaller fixture stays `equal`; recorded as the batch's open sub-item for the coordinator. `cargo test -p xedit-core` 159 passed and `cargo test -p xedit-session --test script` 35 passed on the way. Gates at the tip: `cargo fmt --all --check` clean, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` exit 0, `cargo xtask check` exit 0 (script 3,663 entries, 29 pending), and `cargo test --workspace` green with the corpus tests skipping (without `XEDIT_ORACLE_DIR`); with the environment set, `a_corpus_nif_walks_through_the_data_format_adapter` fails on main's pre-existing `'str' + Boolean` interpreter state (`DfCorpus` line 19), which predates this branch (`crates/xedit-script`, `crates/xedit-core/src/implementation` and `tests/script.rs` did not change between the branch point and the tip) and is recorded, not part of this batch.
11. **Skills and docs** (Sonnet 5.5, after all): the `writing-xedit-scripts` skill, `using-xedit-cli` extended with `xedit script run|list|check`, README, ledger, map, gate evidence.
